**[00:34:19.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000681)

**[00:34:33.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000727)

**[00:34:34.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000728)

**[00:34:37.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000729)

**[00:34:41.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000731)  
the only

**[00:34:42.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000732)  
the only

**[00:34:57.560] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000742)  
For example, Mr. Chairman, Mr. President,

**[00:35:01.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000743)  
the

**[00:35:03.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000744)  
the main ponente, the

**[00:35:06.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000745)  
main decision says

**[00:35:08.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000746)  
that the BICAM

**[00:35:09.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000747)  
can only deal with disagreeing provisions.

**[00:35:13.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000748)  
But if you look, for example, at the concurring decision,

**[00:35:17.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000749)  
concurring opinion

**[00:35:19.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000750)  
of the chief, then Chief Justice

**[00:35:23.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000751)  
Jasmundo,

**[00:35:24.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000752)  
he disagrees.

**[00:35:26.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000753)  
If you look at the

**[00:35:27.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000754)

**[00:35:32.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000755)  
it's four more.

**[00:35:33.660] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000756)  
So they're referring to the Tolentino

**[00:35:36.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000757)  
decision which said that,

**[00:35:39.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000758)  
you know,

**[00:35:40.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000759)  
the house can say,

**[00:35:41.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000760)  
I want letter A.

**[00:35:43.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000761)  
The Senate can say estan Together B,

**[00:35:45.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000762)  
but the BICAM can say no,

**[00:35:47.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000763)  
it's AB.

**[00:35:49.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000764)  
The BIACOM can say it's A.

**[00:35:51.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000765)  
It can come up with a little bit completely something new.

**[00:35:54.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000766)  
And say it's a C.

**[00:35:55.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000767)  
It's C.

**[00:35:55.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000768)  
The reason I ask this, Mr. Chair, is that if we don't consider that, we might have to disagree completely with the House.

**[00:36:05.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000769)

**[00:36:15.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000770)

**[00:36:19.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000771)  
So I know there are probably six or seven, eight parts of the decision.

**[00:36:27.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000772)

**[00:36:30.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000773)

**[00:36:35.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000774)

**[00:36:39.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000775)

**[00:36:41.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000776)

It is both an honor and a great responsibility

**[00:40:45.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000852)  
to lead the Senate Committee on Finance

**[00:40:48.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000853)  
in the review and review of the bill.

**[00:40:48.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000854)  
and deliberation of the proposed 2027 General Appropriations Bill.

**[00:40:54.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000855)  
This representation would like to express

**[00:40:56.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000856)  
his sincere gratitude to the Senate President,

**[00:41:00.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000857)  
Wingat Shajan, for his trust and confidence

**[00:41:03.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000858)  
in delegating this noble task

**[00:41:05.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000859)  
of shepherding this very important piece of legislation.

**[00:41:09.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000860)  
It is my sincere hope that our collective efforts

**[00:41:11.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000861)  
in refining the proposed 2027 National Budget

**[00:41:15.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000862)  
will not only improve the lives of our people next year,

**[00:41:18.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000863)  
but be instrumental in shaping a better future

**[00:41:22.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000864)  
for every Filipino.

**[00:41:24.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000865)  
The budget deliberations this year

**[00:41:25.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000866)  
have been nothing short of challenging.

**[00:41:28.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000867)  
In the middle of an impeachment trial,

**[00:41:30.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000868)  
somehow, someway, we were able to squeeze in our briefings

**[00:41:33.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000869)  
in an alleged and already tight schedule,

**[00:41:37.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000870)  
not to mention the very tight fiscal space.

**[00:41:40.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000871)  
I would like to take this opportunity

**[00:41:42.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000872)  
to thank our distinguished Vice Chairpersons

**[00:41:45.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000873)  
who took on the duty.

**[00:41:47.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000874)  
Together with their staff,

**[00:41:48.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000875)  
the dedicated Senate employees,

**[00:41:51.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000876)  
and most especially,

**[00:41:52.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000877)  
the men and women of the Legislative Budget Research

**[00:41:55.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000878)  
and Monitoring Office,

**[00:41:57.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000879)  
or the LBRMO,

**[00:41:58.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000880)  
who are worked alongside with us,

**[00:42:01.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000881)  
sacrificing their holidays and weekends.

**[00:42:04.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000882)  
And not to mention, Mr. President,

**[00:42:06.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000883)  
that during the ASEAN week,

**[00:42:09.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000884)  
the LBRMO staff even had to bring their computers

**[00:42:14.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000885)  
to the borrowed CA office in BGC.

**[00:42:18.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000886)  
About 40 of them worked for the rest of the week

**[00:42:22.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000887)  
so that there will be no delays

**[00:42:24.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000888)  
and we can finish the budget on time.

**[00:42:26.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000889)  
Maraming salamat po sa inyong sipag,

**[00:42:29.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000890)  
tsaga, at sakripisyo.

**[00:42:31.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000891)  
Your hard work and dedication have been invaluable

**[00:42:34.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000892)  
in bringing us to this stage of the budget process.

**[00:42:38.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000893)  
Mr. President,

**[00:42:39.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000894)  
when I took on the role of Chairperson

**[00:42:41.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000895)  
of the Committee on Finance,

**[00:42:43.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000896)  
I've been very vocal from the beginning.

**[00:42:45.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000897)  
We do not intend to get caught up too much

**[00:42:47.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000898)  
on the complexities of numerics,

**[00:42:48.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000899)  
numerical formulas in the budget process.

**[00:42:51.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000900)  
Our message has been clear and basic.

**[00:42:54.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000901)  
Yes, we should go back to basics.

**[00:42:57.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000902)  
Basic services, that is.

**[00:42:59.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000903)  
Before we focus on complicated matters,

**[00:43:01.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000904)  
we should focus on what limited resources

**[00:43:04.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000905)  
we have in ensuring that the basic needs

**[00:43:06.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000906)  
of our people are provided.

**[00:43:09.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000907)  
But, Mr. President,

**[00:43:10.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000908)  
addressing these basic needs

**[00:43:11.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000909)  
is only one part of the equation.

**[00:43:14.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000910)  
We also need to restore economic momentum.

**[00:43:16.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000911)  
We need to accelerate the implementation

**[00:43:18.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000912)  
of infrastructure projects,

**[00:43:21.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000913)  
both national and local,

**[00:43:23.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000914)  
that will stimulate economic activity

**[00:43:25.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000915)  
and lay down the foundation for long-term growth.

**[00:43:29.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000916)  
We cannot sustain delivering basic services

**[00:43:31.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000917)  
without a strong economy.

**[00:43:32.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000918)  
And no one will appreciate economic growth

**[00:43:36.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000919)  
if it will not translate to better basic services.

**[00:43:40.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000920)  
This, Mr. President,

**[00:43:42.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000921)  
is the direction we want to pursue

**[00:43:43.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000922)  
in the 2027 National Budget,

**[00:43:47.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000923)  
Back to Basics.

**[00:43:48.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000924)  
But with a clear vision

**[00:43:49.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000925)  
for economic recovery and nation building.

**[00:43:53.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000926)  
Ginong, Pangulo,

**[00:43:54.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000927)  
nanindigan po ako na dapat mumuhunan

**[00:43:56.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000928)  
ang pamalaan sa pinakamalagang yaman

**[00:43:59.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000929)  
o asset ng ating bansa.

**[00:44:01.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000930)  
Yun ang kanyang mamamayan.

**[00:44:04.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000931)  
Bilang author at principal sponsor

**[00:44:06.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000932)  
ng Universal Health Care Act,

**[00:44:08.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000933)  
itinataguin ko po ang pagtutuon na padsin

**[00:44:11.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000934)  
sa kalusugan ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:44:15.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000935)  
Naniniwala po tayo

**[00:44:16.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000936)  
ng isang malusog na Pilipino,

**[00:44:18.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000937)  
isang Pilipino na makabag-aambag

**[00:44:20.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000938)  
sa pagpapaunlad ng kanyang bansa.

**[00:44:24.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000939)  
Kaya po sinigurado natin

**[00:44:25.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000940)  
na mapupondohan ang mga programa

**[00:44:28.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000941)  
at proyekto ng Department of Health

**[00:44:30.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000942)  
pati na rin ang attached agencies

**[00:44:32.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000943)  
at corporations nito.

**[00:44:34.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000944)  
Sa versyon ng Senado,

**[00:44:35.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000945)  
naglaan po tayo ng kabuang

**[00:44:37.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000946)  
P378.6 billion.

**[00:44:41.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000947)  
Kasama po rito ang halos

**[00:44:42.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000948)  
P3 billion na dagdagpondo

**[00:44:44.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000949)  
para po sa preventive health services

**[00:44:47.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000950)  
tulad ng health promotion,

**[00:44:48.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000951)  
at procurement ng mga supply

**[00:44:50.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000952)  
para sa immunization,

**[00:44:51.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000953)  
nutrition,

**[00:44:52.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000954)  
at responsible parenting.

**[00:44:54.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000955)  
Nakapaglaan din tayo

**[00:44:56.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000956)  
ng P5.4 billion

**[00:44:58.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000957)  
para sa health...

**[00:45:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000958)  
Facilities Enhancement Program, or HFEP, o ang programang may kinalaman sa construction at improvement ng mga ospital,

**[00:45:08.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000959)  
pati na rin ang mga medical equipment na para sa diagnostics at treatment ng mga pasyente.

**[00:45:14.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000960)  
Ang gusto nga natin, mas palaksid pa ang kakayahan ng mga provincial at ilang locally funded hospitals.

**[00:45:22.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000961)  
Magiging maayos ang healthcare service delivery network at hindi na kinakailangan ng ating mga kababayan na lumos pa ng Maynila upang magpagamot.

**[00:45:32.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000962)  
Makatutulong ito para ma-decongest ang ating mga APEX hospitals, hitting two birds with one stone, ika nga.

**[00:45:40.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000963)  
Ngunit hindi po sapat na paramihin lamang ang ating mga pampublikong ospital o pagandahin ang kanilang mga pasilidad.

**[00:45:48.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000964)  
Kailangan din natin tiyakin na may sapat na pondo ang Peel Health,

**[00:45:52.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000965)  
upang mas marami Pilipino ang makinabang sa mas malawak na health benefits.

**[00:45:57.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000966)  
Kaya naman, dinagdagan po ng inyong committee and finance ng 19.8 billion ang pondo ng Peel Health

**[00:46:03.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000967)  
upang maibigay ng buo ang earmarked funds mula sa syntax collections para sa 2027.

**[00:46:12.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000968)  
Dahil dito ay aabot na sa 94.2 billion ang subsidiya ng Peel Health para sa indirect contributors

**[00:46:19.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000969)  
at sa pagpapaganda at pagpapalawak ng kaibigan.

**[00:46:22.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000970)  
Gusto ko sana, gusto sana natin na mapataas ang pondo para sa Peel Health hanggang 100 billion.

**[00:46:30.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000971)  
Ngunit naintindihan natin na limited ang ating fiscal space.

**[00:46:35.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000972)  
Ayon sa Peel Health, target nila mapababa ang out-of-pocket health expenses ng ating makababayan

**[00:46:41.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000973)  
mula 41.2% tungo sa 37.8 pagsapit ng 2028.

**[00:46:48.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000974)  
Ito pong dahilan kung bakit mahalagang maibigay natin sa Peel Health ang buong pondong nakalaan dito mula sa syntaxes.

**[00:46:57.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000975)  
Sa ganitong paraan, mas maraming benepisyo ang may bibigay ng Peel Health

**[00:47:00.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000976)  
at mababawasan ang gastos na kailangan hugutin ng ating makababayan mula sa sarili nila ng bulsa kapag sila ay nagkakasakit.

**[00:47:11.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000977)  
Mahalaga rin po ang pagtuunan ng pansin ang mental health na sa pagdaan ng mga taon ay nakita natin na dapat bigyang atensyon,

**[00:47:18.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000978)  
lalo na sa pag-usad ng modernong panahon.

**[00:47:22.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000979)  
Kaugnay nito, sinigurado po natin na mula sa mental health program ng DOH,

**[00:47:27.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000980)  
malinaw na magagamit ang 300 milyon para sa pagbili at paghahatid ng mahalagang gamot,

**[00:47:34.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000981)  
alinsunod na rin po mismo sa Mental Health Act.

**[00:47:38.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000982)  
Sa ilalim din po ng Senate version ng budget, kapansin-pansin naman po ang pagbabaan ng medical assistance program,

**[00:47:45.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000983)  
previously known as the Medical Assistance to Indigent Infant,

**[00:47:48.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000984)  
Financially Incapacitated Patients, o ang MAIFIP.

**[00:47:52.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000985)  
Ngunit hindi po dapat ikabahalan ang ating makababayan.

**[00:47:56.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000986)  
Hindi ito nangangahulugan na mababawasan ang tulong para sa mga pasyente.

**[00:48:01.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000987)  
Sa halip, malaking bahagi ng pondo ay direktang nating inilaan sa mga ospital

**[00:48:07.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000988)  
upang mas mapalawak at maayos at maipatupad ang Zero Balance Billing Program.

**[00:48:15.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000989)  
Basic lang po ang gusto natin dito.

**[00:48:18.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000990)  
Mapawi ang alalahanin ng bawat pasyente,

**[00:48:22.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000991)  
hindi na niya dapat isipin kung saan kukuha ng pambayad sa pagpapagamot

**[00:48:27.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000992)  
at ang tanging iintindihin na lamang niya ay ang pagpapagaling.

**[00:48:32.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000993)  
Mababawasan po ang pangangailangan pumila at tumapit sa kung saan saang tanggapan

**[00:48:38.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000994)  
para makahingi ng mga guarantee letters na pambayad sa hospital bills.

**[00:48:43.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000995)  
Mr. President, while we take healthcare accessible,

**[00:48:47.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000996)  
we make healthcare accessible,

**[00:48:49.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000997)  
it is equally important that we ensure that our people are well-nourished.

**[00:48:54.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000998)  
Having a healthcare system that we can always rely on is good,

**[00:48:57.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000999)  
but we believe that prevention is still better than cure.

**[00:49:02.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001000)  
A pandemic is similar to a natural disaster that is almost beyond our control.

**[00:49:07.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001001)  
Though difficult, what we can prevent is hunger and malnutrition.

**[00:49:11.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001002)  
We must see to it that every Filipino is able to eat every day.

**[00:49:15.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001003)  
This will only be possible...

**[00:49:17.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001004)  
We continue to push for food security.

**[00:49:20.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001005)  
For this to come to fruition, we must ensure the productivity of our farmlands for 2027.

**[00:49:26.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001006)  
The Department of Agriculture and its attached agencies and corporations,

**[00:49:31.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001007)  
including irrigation support, will have a combined budget of around P251 billion.

**[00:49:37.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001008)  
This will ensure that our farmers receive the support they need,

**[00:49:40.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001009)  
from sowing seeds, fertilization of crops, to harvesting and selling of goods.

**[00:49:47.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001010)  
Your Committee has also re-aligned more than P4 billion for the National Rice Program to help boost rice production.

**[00:49:53.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001011)  
And with the continuing threat of drought and El Niño,

**[00:49:57.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001012)  
we have provided P1 billion for the DA's Quick Response Fund, or the QRF,

**[00:50:02.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001013)  
and P250 million for the Agri-Recover Program to help farmers affected by these conditions.

**[00:50:08.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001014)  
We cannot stop Super El Niño from happening,

**[00:50:11.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001015)  
but we can prepare and be ready to respond the moment it makes its presence felt.

**[00:50:17.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001016)  
Mr. President, this is through the efforts of our distinguished Vice-Chair,

**[00:50:22.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001017)  
Senator Francis Pangilinan,

**[00:50:24.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001018)  
who is very knowledgeable of the needs and challenges of the agricultural sector,

**[00:50:29.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001019)  
being an organic farmer himself.

**[00:50:32.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001020)  
Thank you, sir.

**[00:50:34.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001021)  
In relation to this,

**[00:50:36.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001022)  
National Irrigation Administration will receive P47.5 billion in subsidy

**[00:50:42.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001023)  
to allow the continued flow of water for farming.

**[00:50:45.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001024)  
However,

**[00:50:47.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001025)  
the amount of water is only one of the many demands of our farmers.

**[00:50:52.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001026)  
Para sa mga vegetable farmers ng Benguet at Mountain Province,

**[00:50:56.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001027)  
bagama't nabibiyayaan ng mga aning gulay,

**[00:51:00.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001028)  
kadalasan naman ay

**[00:51:01.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001029)  
inaabutan ng pagkabulok ang mga kalakal dahil sa

**[00:51:07.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001030)  
kawalan ng angkop na lugar upang pag-imbakan.

**[00:51:10.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001031)  
Buong pagmamalaki po nating ibinabalita ang paglalaan natin ng mahigit isang bilyon

**[00:51:13.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001032)  
para sa pagpapatayo ng Benguet Agri-Pinoy Trading Center,

**[00:51:17.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001033)  
at Cold Storage Facility sa Barlig, Mountain Province.

**[00:51:21.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001034)  
Ito po ay magsisilbing solusyon sa matagal nang problema ng ating mga kababayang vegetable farmers.

**[00:51:29.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001035)  
Maliban po ito sa 200 million na inilaan pa sa pagpapatayo naman ng cold storage facility sa Oriental Mindoro.

**[00:51:37.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001036)  
Sa halip na mabulok at masayang ang ating pinaghirapang ani,

**[00:51:41.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001037)  
ito ay maiiimbak nang mas matagal

**[00:51:45.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001038)  
at sa angkop na kondisyon,

**[00:51:47.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001039)  
ang ilang produkto ay maaaring tumagal ng hanggang anim na buwan.

**[00:51:51.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001040)  
Mabibigyan din nito ang ating mga magsasaka ng pagkakataong maibenta ang kanilang mga produkto sa mas magandang presyo.

**[00:51:58.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001041)  
Hindi lamang po ang ating mga magsasaka makikinabang dito.

**[00:52:02.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001042)  
by reducing post-harvest losses,

**[00:52:05.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001043)  
we can help maintain a stable food supply and prevent sudden increases in food prices,

**[00:52:10.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001044)  
particularly during calamities and periods of shortage.

**[00:52:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001045)  
Sa madaling salita,

**[00:52:16.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001046)  
Mr. President,

**[00:52:18.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001047)  
mas kaunting nasasayang at mas malaking kita para sa ating mga magsasaka,

**[00:52:23.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001048)  
at matatag na supply ng abot-kayang pagkain para sa ating mga kababayan.

**[00:52:29.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001049)  
Kasabay ng pagpapalakas ng lokal na produksyon,

**[00:52:30.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001050)  
dapat ding maging maingat tayo

**[00:52:33.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001051)  
sa pag-angkat ng mga produktong agrikultural.

**[00:52:38.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001052)  
Importation should only be allowed when there is no local harvest

**[00:52:42.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001053)  
or when domestic supply is genuinely insufficient.

**[00:52:46.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001054)  
Hindi makatarungan na sa panahon ng anihan

**[00:52:49.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001055)  
ay makikipagkompetensya pa ang ating mga magsasaka

**[00:52:52.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001056)  
sa mas murang imported na produkto

**[00:52:55.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001057)  
na nagiging dahilan ng pagbaba ng presyo ng kanilang ani at pagkalugi.

**[00:53:00.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001058)  
Dapat nakabatay sa aktwal na datos ng produksyon,

**[00:53:04.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001059)  
imbentaryo at panahon ng anihan,

**[00:53:06.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001060)  
ang anumang desisyon sa importasyon.

**[00:53:09.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001061)  
Kung may sapat na lokal na ani,

**[00:53:12.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001062)  
unahin nating bilhin

**[00:53:13.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001063)  
at suportahan ang produkto ng ating mga magsasaka.

**[00:53:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001064)  
Importation must address real shortages

**[00:53:20.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001065)  
that undermine the livelihoods of the very farmers who are trying to help.

**[00:53:26.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001066)  
We advocate for food security

**[00:53:28.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001067)  
because we believe that every Filipino should be well fed.

**[00:53:31.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001068)  
A Filipino that eats regularly and is well-nourished will do better in life.

**[00:53:37.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001069)  
But before that,

**[00:53:38.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001070)  
we want our people to do good in school.

**[00:53:41.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001071)  
Mr. President,

**[00:53:42.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001072)  
learners from Kinder to Grade 1

**[00:53:45.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001073)  
will benefit from the Department of Education's school-based feeding program.

**[00:53:50.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001074)  
Teachers will provide the food for their brains.

**[00:53:53.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001075)  
DepEd will provide the food for their tummies.

**[00:53:55.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001076)  
DepEd will allocate 11 billion for its school-based feeding program,

**[00:54:00.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001077)  
which shall form part of its total 2027 budget,

**[00:54:03.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001078)  
amounting to 915.4 billion.

**[00:54:07.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001079)  
We would like to thank our Vice Chair,

**[00:54:10.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001080)  
Senator Bambachino,

**[00:54:11.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001081)  
for pushing for the funding for this program

**[00:54:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001082)  
that will contribute to the growth and development of our pupils

**[00:54:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001083)  
and help prevent stunting.

**[00:54:19.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001084)  
Again, thank you very much to Senator Bambachino.

**[00:54:23.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001085)  
For higher education,

**[00:54:25.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001086)  
we have allocated a total of 138.2 billion

**[00:54:30.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001087)  
for our state universities and colleges or SUCs.

**[00:54:34.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001088)  
This will support their operations,

**[00:54:36.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001089)  
academic programs,

**[00:54:38.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001090)  
and the continued construction,

**[00:54:40.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001091)  
rehabilitation,

**[00:54:41.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001092)  
and improvement of school buildings and other facilities.

**[00:54:44.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001093)  
Included in this allocation is 7.5 million for each SUC

**[00:54:49.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001094)  
under the Smart Campus Project,

**[00:54:51.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001095)  
which aims to improve internet connectivity

**[00:54:53.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001096)  
and support digital learning across more than 100 campuses nationwide.

**[00:54:59.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001097)  
Dear colleagues,

**[00:55:01.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001098)  
this program will make information technology more accessible to our students.

**[00:55:05.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001099)  
This was advocated by my brother and seatmate,

**[00:55:08.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001100)  
Vice Chair,

**[00:55:09.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001101)  
Senator Joel Villanueva.

**[00:55:11.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001102)  
Thank you very much,

**[00:55:12.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001103)  
Senator Joel.

**[00:55:13.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001104)  
If we keep our students with the proper learning tools,

**[00:55:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001105)  
they will be ready to find jobs

**[00:55:19.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001106)  
and contribute to our economy.

**[00:55:21.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001107)  
But simultaneously,

**[00:55:23.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001108)  
we find ways to generate jobs,

**[00:55:25.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001109)  
to accommodate our graduates and job seekers.

**[00:55:28.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001110)  
This is where infrastructure development comes in,

**[00:55:32.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001111)  
more opportunities for our people.

**[00:55:35.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001112)  
Nung sumambulat po,

**[00:55:37.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001113)  
ang flood control scandal,

**[00:55:39.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001114)  
perhaps the greatest corruption scandal

**[00:55:42.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001115)  
in the history of the Philippines.

**[00:55:44.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001116)  
Halos tumigol po ang lahat

**[00:55:46.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001117)  
ng ating infrastructure projects.

**[00:55:48.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001118)  
Kasabay din ito ang paghinto ng oportunidad

**[00:55:51.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001119)  
para sa marami sa ating mga kababayan.

**[00:55:54.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001120)  
Dahil sa iskandalo,

**[00:55:56.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001121)  
naging mabagal ang infrastructure spending

**[00:55:59.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001122)  
at labis na naapektuhan ang kumpiyansa ng mga investors.

**[00:56:04.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001123)  
Naniniwala po tayo,

**[00:56:06.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001124)  
ginong Pangulo,

**[00:56:07.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001125)  
na hindi dapat tumigil ang lahat

**[00:56:09.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001126)  
dahil sa flood control scandal.

**[00:56:11.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001127)  
Hayaan po natin magpatuloy ang mga investigasyon,

**[00:56:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001128)  
ang pagsampan ng mga kaso,

**[00:56:16.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001129)  
at pagpapanagot sa lahat ng mga sangkot.

**[00:56:19.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001130)  
Sa bahagi po naman po natin,

**[00:56:22.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001131)  
dapat na magpatuloy tayo

**[00:56:24.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001132)  
sa pagsusulong ng infrastructure development

**[00:56:27.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001133)  
na makapaglilika ng hanap buhay

**[00:56:29.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001134)  
at maghahatid ng ginhawa sa ating bayan.

**[00:56:33.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001135)  
Mr. President,

**[00:56:34.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001136)  
for many years,

**[00:56:35.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001137)  
infrastructure development had to compete

**[00:56:38.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001138)  
with other pressing fiscal obligations,

**[00:56:40.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001139)  
including debt payments.

**[00:56:42.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001140)  
As investments in critical infrastructure

**[00:56:45.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001141)  
were constrained or deferred,

**[00:56:47.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001142)  
industries that depend on reliable roads,

**[00:56:50.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001143)  
ports, transportation,

**[00:56:52.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001144)  
and other public facilities also felt the impact.

**[00:56:55.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001145)  
Delayed projects mean delayed investments,

**[00:56:58.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001146)  
fewer jobs,

**[00:56:59.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001147)  
and missed opportunities for economic growth.

**[00:57:02.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001148)  
Ito po ang dahilan kung bakit

**[00:57:04.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001149)  
malaga ang ating panukalang

**[00:57:06.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001150)  
Master Plan for Infrastructure and National Development

**[00:57:09.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001151)  
or BIND Act.

**[00:57:11.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001152)  
Layunin nito magkaroon ng malinaw at pangmatagalang plano

**[00:57:15.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001153)  
para sa mga pangunahing infrastruktura

**[00:57:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001154)  
upang ang maproyekto ay nakabatay sa tunay na pangangailangan,

**[00:57:22.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001155)  
kahandaan at benepisyo sa ekonomiya,

**[00:57:25.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001156)  
hindi sa pansarili at pansamantalang interes.

**[00:57:29.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001157)  
We must accelerate growth without sacrificing accountability.

**[00:57:32.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001158)  
Bilisan natin ang pagpapatubad ng mahalagang proyekto,

**[00:57:39.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001159)  
ngunit yakinak natin na kumpleto ang dokumento,

**[00:57:42.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001160)  
malinaw ang pagpili ng proyekto,

**[00:57:45.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001161)  
at bawat pisong ginagastos ay may tunay

**[00:57:49.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001162)  
na panginabang sa ating mga kababayan.

**[00:57:52.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001163)  
Ang ating kailangan,

**[00:57:54.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001164)  
mga proyektong may silbi at mga proyektong magsisilbi.

**[00:57:58.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001165)  
Para po sa Department of Public Works and Labor,

**[00:58:01.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001166)  
o Public Works and Highways, o DPWH,

**[00:58:04.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001167)  
makakatanggap po sila ng higit 587 bilyon para sa susunod na taon.

**[00:58:11.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001168)  
Nakapaloob po rito ang iba't ibang infrastructure projects

**[00:58:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001169)  
na maglalatlag ng ating landas patungo sa kaunlaran.

**[00:58:18.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001170)  
Ibinileg po ng inyong komiti ang 33.097 bilyon na bahagi ng pondong binawas

**[00:58:25.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001171)  
para sa Foreign Assisted Projects, o FAPs,

**[00:58:29.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001172)  
kabilang sa mahalangang proyektong nais natin ipagpatuloy

**[00:58:33.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001173)  
ang Bataan-Cavite Interlink Bridge, Laguna Lakeshore Road Network,

**[00:58:39.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001174)  
at Pasig-Marikina River Channel Improvement Project.

**[00:58:44.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001175)  
Upang mapawi ang pangamba o agang-abang ng ating mga kababayan,

**[00:58:49.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001176)  
naniniligan po tayo na dapat ay tapusin muna ang mahindi patapos na flood management program.

**[00:58:55.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001177)  
Dapat bigyang prioridad ang mga ito,

**[00:58:57.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001178)  
kaysa magpanukala ng mga panibago.

**[00:59:00.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001179)  
Sa ating mga manggagawa na hindi lamang instrumental sa infrastructure development,

**[00:59:06.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001180)  
bagos ay matuturing din natin na haligin ang ating ekonomiya,

**[00:59:10.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001181)  
hindi po namin kayo nalilimutan.

**[00:59:13.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001182)  
Ang kabawang budget ng Department of Labor and Employment, o DOLE,

**[00:59:19.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001183)  
para sa susunod na taon ay nagkakahalaga ng 53.8 bilyon.

**[00:59:24.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001184)  
Ito po ay para sa kapakanan ng...

**[00:59:26.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001185)  
at proteksyon ng mga manggagawa.

**[00:59:32.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001186)  
Nagbigay din na karagdagang budget ang inyong komitee ng 250 milyon para sa Action Fund

**[00:59:37.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001187)  
ng Department of Migrant Workers,

**[00:59:39.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001188)  
at 600 milyon para sa Emergency Repatriation Fund

**[00:59:45.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001189)  
ng Overseas Workers Welfare Administration, or OWA.

**[00:59:50.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001190)  
Gusto po nating tiyakin na may sapat na pondo para agad na matulungan,

**[00:59:54.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001191)  
at mapawi ang ating mga OFW kapag may krisis sa bansa ang kanilang pinagtatrabahuhan.

**[01:00:01.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001192)  
Malaki po ang sakripisyo ng ating mga OFW para sa kanilang pamilya at sa ating bansa.

**[01:00:07.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001193)  
Kaya kapag sila naman ang nangailangan ng tulong, dapat nandyan agad ang ating gobyerno para umalalay sa kanila.

**[01:00:14.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001194)  
Malaga po mabanggit ang Sugarcane Workers Assistance Scholarship para sa mga sugarcane industry workers

**[01:00:21.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001195)  
at sugarcane farmers at kanilang mga dependents na makakatulong sa pagpapagaan ng bayarin sa matrikula.

**[01:00:29.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001196)  
Pagkakaloob ng stipend at maging thesis allowance, 20 milyon ay nilaan natin sa programa ito na nakalagak sa DOLE-OSEC.

**[01:00:40.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001197)  
Parang po sa ating makawani at mamagagawa sa pamalaan,

**[01:00:45.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001198)  
isinama po natin sa ating Senate version ng 2027 Budget ang pagdagdag ng 1,000 piso para sa buwanang Personal Economic Relief Allowance o PERA

**[01:00:56.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001199)  
kasama po na magbebukas.

**[01:00:59.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001200)  
Ang pinipisyo mula rito ang ating mga guro na katulad din ng mga manggagawa at mga empleyado sa pribadong sektor ay naapekton din ng pagtaas sa mga bilihin.

**[01:01:09.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001201)  
Ito po ay kaalinsabay ng ating panukala na itaas ang sahod ng mga guro na dalangin natin ay ganap ng maisabatas.

**[01:01:19.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001202)  
To help our fellow Filipinos get to work faster and turn home safely, we will continue improving our transportation system.

**[01:01:29.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001203)  
We restored 4.7 billion GOP counterpart and another 15 billion for the continued construction of the Metro Manila Subway Project

**[01:01:39.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001204)  
and another 15 billion for the North-South Commuter Railway, both under the JICA load.

**[01:01:47.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001205)  
To further improve the commuting experience for so many of our people on the road,

**[01:01:51.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001206)  
the Senate version of the budget also provides funding for the NCR Busway Project and for the Active Transport Bike Share System,

**[01:01:59.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001207)  
and Safe Pathways Program.

**[01:02:01.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001208)  
In all, funding for these two important initiatives amounts collectively to 146.5 billion.

**[01:02:11.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001209)  
Taken together, these projects will make daily travel faster and more convenient, especially for our workers.

**[01:02:18.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001210)  
We have likewise ensured adequate funding for the construction and rehabilitation for airports and seaports nationwide.

**[01:02:24.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001211)  
In particular, we allocated an additional 600 million pesos,

**[01:02:29.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001212)  
for various airport development and improvement projects across the country,

**[01:02:34.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001213)  
with the aim of providing our Kababayans with safer, more reliable, and more convenient air travel

**[01:02:40.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001214)  
while enhancing connectivity and supporting economic growth in our regions.

**[01:02:44.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001215)  
All these initiatives form part of the 294.57 billion budget of the Department of Transportation, or DOTR, for 2027.

**[01:02:54.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001216)  
As we continue to improve transportation,

**[01:02:57.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001217)  
we also want more Filipino families to

**[01:02:58.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001219)  
have decent and affordable homes.

**[01:03:01.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001220)  
For 2027, the Department of Human Settlements and Urban Development, or DSHUD,

**[01:03:06.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001221)  
and its attached agencies or corporations will receive a total of 6.4 billion.

**[01:03:11.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001222)  
This includes your committee's augmentation of 1.005 billion for the National Housing Corporation and Socialized Housing Finance Corporation.

**[01:03:23.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001223)  
While the budget for the expanded 4PH program was reduced to 600 million in the House budget,

**[01:03:28.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001224)  
we have provided an additional 504.8 billion for the Community Mortgage Program under the Social Housing Finance Corporation.

**[01:03:38.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001234)  
We have also added P400 million for the housing projects under the National Housing Authority or NHA.

**[01:03:46.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001235)  
Mr. President, gusto lang naman natin na mas maraming pamilyang Pilipino magkaroon ng sariling bahay.

**[01:03:53.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001236)  
Para naman po sa ating mga kumbaya na naghihikahos,

**[01:03:56.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001237)  
makakaasa po kayo ng kaliga mula sa Department of Social Welfare Development o DSWD.

**[01:04:02.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001238)  
Para po sa susunod na taon, ang kabuang budget po ng DSWD ay P253.3 billion.

**[01:04:10.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001239)  
Ang Assistance to Individuals in Crisis Situations o AICS magkakahalaga ng P48.1 billion,

**[01:04:17.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001240)  
mas mababa ng halos P10 billion kumpara sa nasa original na General Appropriations Bill.

**[01:04:25.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001241)  
Samantalang, P99 billion ang pondo para sa Pantawid Pamilyang Pilipino Program or 4Ps.

**[01:04:32.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001242)  
Ngunit hindi lamang po panay-pamigay o ayudang DSWD.

**[01:04:37.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001243)  
Mayroon din po silang Sustainable Livelihood Program o SLP.

**[01:04:42.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001244)  
Ito ang kanilang Community-Based Capacity Building Program

**[01:04:45.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001245)  
para mapabuti ang antasang pamumuhay ng mga hihirap at vulnerable.

**[01:04:51.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001246)  
Pagkakaluban po ng puhunan ang isang grupo ng mga mamamayan sa isang komunidad na nais makapagnegosyo.

**[01:04:59.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001247)  
Kailangan lamang po ang na-accredited ng DSWD

**[01:05:02.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001248)  
sa kanilang grupo o kooperatiba.

**[01:05:05.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001249)  
P4.2 billion ang pondo na inilaan po natin para sa programang ito.

**[01:05:11.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001250)  
Kasama rin po ang DSWD sa mga ahensya na naghahanda para sa inaasahang paghagupit ng Super El Nino sa ating bansa.

**[01:05:20.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001251)  
Para sa pagpaparating na tagtuyot,

**[01:05:24.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001252)  
nagkakahalaga po ng P1.4 billion ang nakatakdang panggastos ng DSWD para sa iba't ibang mga programa

**[01:05:32.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001253)  
pagpapadaloy ng tulong.

**[01:05:35.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001254)  
Bungo naman po ito ng pagsusumikat ng ating Vice Chair Centaurisa Ontiveros

**[01:05:39.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001255)  
na nagsusulong at namapondohan ang mga programang naglalayon may padama

**[01:05:44.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001256)  
ang pag-aalaga at pag-aaruga sa ating mga kababayan na nangangailangan.

**[01:05:50.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001257)  
Muli, maraming salamat, Centaurisa.

**[01:05:53.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001258)  
Para naman po alalaya ng ating mga marginalized at disadvantaged na mga kababayan,

**[01:05:59.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001259)  
makatatanggap po ang National Anti-Poverty Commission o NAPA.

**[01:06:02.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001260)  
Muli, maraming salamat, Centaurisa.

**[01:06:05.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001261)  
Nagkakahalaga ng P559.9 milyon pesos.

**[01:06:09.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001262)  
Ang pondo pong ito ay makatutulong sa kanilang mga proyekto at mga programa

**[01:06:14.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001263)  
kaugnay ng poverty reduction at mga social reform programs.

**[01:06:20.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001264)  
As we work hard to ensure the provision of fundamental needs,

**[01:06:24.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001265)  
we must also continue to make efforts for the protection and safety for people from threats domestic and foreign.

**[01:06:31.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001266)  
For the Philippine National Police or PNP,

**[01:06:34.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001267)  
your committee has augmented its 2027 proposed budget by 2.9 billion for a total of 245.2 billion.

**[01:06:45.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001268)  
This would strengthen police operations, upgrade their facilities,

**[01:06:49.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001269)  
and back their fight against terrorism and other security threats.

**[01:06:54.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001270)  
One of the major initiatives that we funded within the PNP's budget

**[01:06:58.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001271)  
is the completion of the Police Regional Office,

**[01:07:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001272)  
which is in the newly created Negros Island Region.

**[01:07:05.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001273)  
As both author and principal sponsor of the Negros Island Region Act,

**[01:07:10.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001274)  
which we passed last 2024,

**[01:07:12.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001275)  
we are pleased to finally see the effects of our law coming to full fruition

**[01:07:17.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001276)  
through a more focused and rooted national government presence for all Negrenses.

**[01:07:23.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001277)  
We also enhanced the budget of the Bureau of Fire Protection or BFP by 922 million,

**[01:07:29.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001278)  
bringing it to 39.3 billion.

**[01:07:32.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001279)  
This will allow the hiring of 2,000 more firefighters,

**[01:07:36.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001280)  
the purchase of ambulances,

**[01:07:38.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001281)  
and improvements in emergency response.

**[01:07:41.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001282)  
Meanwhile, the Bureau of Jail Management and Penology or the BJMP

**[01:07:46.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001283)  
would get 34.4 billion to further upgrade the administration and conditions of our local jails.

**[01:07:54.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001284)  
These are all part of the proposed 2027 budget of the Department of the Interior and Local Government or DILG,

**[01:08:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001285)  
which our committee has boosted by more than 4 billion pesos,

**[01:08:05.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001286)  
bringing the overall budget of DILG to 334.9 billion.

**[01:08:11.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001287)  
We have always believed in the courage and perseverance that our police and other uniformed personnel possess.

**[01:08:17.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001288)  
What is few, however, are the tools and equipment that will enhance their skills and amplify their capabilities.

**[01:08:25.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001289)  
Mr. President, dear colleagues, this was made possible through the collective efforts of our Senate President,

**[01:08:31.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001290)  
Win Gatchalian, our Vice-Chair, Senator Panfilo Lacson, and his representation.

**[01:08:37.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001291)  
Perhaps there is no other person who fully understands the needs of our police than someone who became Chief PNP himself.

**[01:08:45.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001292)  
This humble representation would like to express its deep gratitude to Senator Lacson

**[01:08:50.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001293)  
for keeping an eagle eye in scrutinizing the proposed 2027 budget as a whole.

**[01:08:57.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001294)  
Maraming salamat po, Sen. Ping.

**[01:08:59.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001295)  
Mr. President, aside from keeping,

**[01:09:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001296)  
our community secure, we must also reinforce the defenses of our country,

**[01:09:05.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001297)  
especially with the continued challenges in the West Philippine Sea.

**[01:09:10.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001298)  
The committee also raised the budget of the Department of National Defense or DND

**[01:09:14.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001299)  
by 1.73 billion to 326.9 billion in 2027.

**[01:09:20.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001300)  
This is on top of the 50 billion already earmarked for the continued modernization of the Armed Forces of the Philippines,

**[01:09:28.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001301)  
which, by the way, is in the program appropriation.

**[01:09:31.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001302)  
This is probably the biggest ever funding for the EMP modernization in the program appropriations at 50 billion pesos.

**[01:09:41.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001303)  
Included in the additional allocation is 750 million for the Philippine Navy,

**[01:09:46.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001304)  
raising its budget to 71.2 billion.

**[01:09:49.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001305)  
The nine key infrastructure projects in the Kalayaan Island Group,

**[01:09:54.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001306)  
including Pag-asa Island, will be funded by this.

**[01:09:57.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001307)  
We intend to upgrade our facilities in Kalayaan,

**[01:10:00.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001308)  
increase our presence in the West,

**[01:10:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001309)  
for the Philippine Coast Guard or the PCG,

**[01:10:05.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001310)  
we augmented its budget to 45.6 billion by adding 146.8 million for the acquisition of training helicopters.

**[01:10:15.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001311)  
Mr. President, the presence of our Navy and Coast Guard brings confidence to our fishermen

**[01:10:19.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001312)  
who only wish to peacefully earn an honest living in the West Philippine Sea,

**[01:10:23.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001313)  
without threat or intimidation.

**[01:10:26.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001314)  
Kalusugan, pagkain, edukasyon, trabaho at siguridad.

**[01:10:30.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001315)  
Yan.

**[01:10:31.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001316)  
At po, ang nais nating pagtuunan ng pansin

**[01:10:34.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001317)  
sa pagbabalangkas at deliberasyon natin ng 2027 National Budget.

**[01:10:40.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001318)  
Back to basics.

**[01:10:41.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001319)  
Unahin natin ang pagsisiguro

**[01:10:43.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001320)  
ng mas maayos at epesyenteng nakakarating sa ating mamamayan,

**[01:10:48.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001321)  
ang basic services.

**[01:10:51.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001322)  
Batayang serbisyo ang batayan natin ng tagumpay.

**[01:10:54.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001323)  
But as I mentioned at the outset,

**[01:10:58.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001324)  
providing basic services is only part of the equation.

**[01:11:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001325)  
We must also push for economic recovery,

**[01:11:04.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001326)  
driven by the accelerated implementation of infrastructure projects,

**[01:11:08.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001327)  
particularly the major flagship projects

**[01:11:11.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001328)  
that are critical to nation-building and long-term economic growth.

**[01:11:16.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001329)  
We must aim for at least 5% to 6% GDP growth

**[01:11:20.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001330)  
by boosting infrastructure development at both the national and local levels.

**[01:11:25.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001331)  
These investments will generate jobs, stimulate economic activity,

**[01:11:31.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001332)  
strengthen local economies,

**[01:11:32.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001333)  
and improve connectivity across the country.

**[01:11:36.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001334)  
At the same time,

**[01:11:37.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001335)  
we must go back to basics and prioritize

**[01:11:40.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001336)  
the essential needs of our people,

**[01:11:42.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001337)  
particularly healthcare.

**[01:11:45.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001338)  
This means strengthening the implementation

**[01:11:46.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001339)  
of the universal healthcare law

**[01:11:49.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001340)  
by increasing government subsidies to field health,

**[01:11:52.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001341)  
expanding healthcare benefits,

**[01:11:54.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001342)  
and improving health facilities nationwide.

**[01:11:58.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001343)  
Mr. President, our goal is clear.

**[01:12:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001344)  
Accelerate economic growth while ensuring that its benefits are felt

**[01:12:05.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001345)  
by ordinary Filipinos through better jobs,

**[01:12:09.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001346)  
accessible healthcare, and improved public services.

**[01:12:13.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001347)  
Sa pagkat sa likod ng bawat kwento,

**[01:12:16.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001348)  
kwenta ay may mga kwento.

**[01:12:19.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001349)  
Mga kwento ng mga magulang na walang pambayad sa ospital

**[01:12:23.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001350)  
para sa kalang anak na may sakit.

**[01:12:25.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001351)  
Mga pamilyang nagtitiis sa gutom dahil walang pambili ng pagkain.

**[01:12:30.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001352)  
Mga kabataang hindi.

**[01:12:31.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001353)  
Mga kabataang hindi makapag-aral dahil sa kahirapan.

**[01:12:34.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001354)  
Mga magsasakang hindi makapagtanim dahil walang sapat na erigasyon.

**[01:12:39.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001355)  
Mga manggagawang napipilitang maglakad ng malayo

**[01:12:42.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001356)  
dahil hindi na kaya ang mahal na pamasahe.

**[01:12:46.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001357)  
At mga manging isdang natatakot pumalaot sa sarili nating dagat

**[01:12:50.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001358)  
dahil sa pangaharas at paggamit ng water cannon na mga dayuhan.

**[01:12:56.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001359)  
Sila po ang dapat lamanin ang ating mga isip at puso

**[01:13:00.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001360)  
sa pagtalaan.

**[01:13:01.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001361)  
at pagdidesisyon sa bawat pisong bubuo sa 2027 National Budget.

**[01:13:09.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001362)  
Dahil sa huli, ang tunay na sukatan ng isang pambansang budget ay hindi kung gaano kalaki ang pondong inilian,

**[01:13:16.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001363)  
kundi kung gaano karaming buhay ang ating mapapabuti.

**[01:13:21.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001364)  
Justice is often depicted as a blindfolded lady holding a scale that tips according to the weight of evidence.

**[01:13:29.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001365)  
The budget process is no different.

**[01:13:33.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001366)  
As legislators, we too hold the scale in our hands, but not to weigh evidence, but to balance the interests.

**[01:13:42.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001367)  
In crafting the 2027 National Budget, we cannot afford to be blind to the harsh realities faced by our people.

**[01:13:51.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001368)  
And while the scales of justice must weigh evidence impartially,

**[01:13:54.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001369)  
the scales of social justice must give greater weight to the needs of those

**[01:13:59.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001370)  
who have the least.

**[01:14:01.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001371)  
Before I end, Mr. President, allow me to highlight a few more significant adjustments and key provisions

**[01:14:07.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001372)  
in your committee report that deserve the attention of this August Chamber.

**[01:14:11.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001373)  
For the Department of Information and Communications Technology, or DICT,

**[01:14:16.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001374)  
their total budget is 8.5 billion, 715.8 million of which will go to the Cybercrime Investigation and Coordinating Center, or CICC,

**[01:14:25.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001375)  
to strengthen their fight against the spread of misinformation,

**[01:14:29.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001376)  
scams, and the protection of our youth from nihilistic, violent extremism online.

**[01:14:36.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001377)  
The internet should be a tool for learning, and cyberspace should be a safe space for everyone,

**[01:14:42.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001378)  
especially for our children.

**[01:14:45.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001379)  
At katulad rin po ng pagpiyak natin ang kaligtasan sa digital space,

**[01:14:50.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001380)  
mahalaga rin pong siguruduhin ang sapat na supply ng ating kuryente.

**[01:14:54.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001381)  
Kaya naman po gusto rin natin pasalamatan si Sen. Pia Caetano

**[01:14:58.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001382)  
sa kanyang masigal.

**[01:15:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001383)  
na kontribusyon bilang Vice Chair

**[01:15:01.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001384)  
para sa sektor ng Energia.

**[01:15:04.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001385)  
Bagamat hindi po natin

**[01:15:06.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001386)  
kayang pondohan

**[01:15:07.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001387)  
ang panukalan 60 billion

**[01:15:09.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001388)  
para sa pagpapatayong

**[01:15:11.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001389)  
pasilidad at pag-iimbak

**[01:15:13.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001390)

**[01:15:16.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001391)  
na sapat para sa

**[01:15:17.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001392)  
90 hanggang 180

**[01:15:19.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001393)  
na araw

**[01:15:21.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001394)  
na pangangailangan ng bansa.

**[01:15:23.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001395)  
Nakapagdagdag naman po tayo

**[01:15:25.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001396)  
ng higit 196 million

**[01:15:27.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001397)  
para sa pagpapalawak

**[01:15:29.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001398)

**[01:15:31.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001399)

**[01:15:33.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001400)  
Program ng National Power Corporation.

Mahalagang hakbang po ito upang

**[01:15:39.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001402)

**[01:15:41.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001403)

**[01:15:45.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001405)

**[01:15:47.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001406)

**[01:48:37.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002311)

**[01:51:50.680] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002407)

**[01:51:58.640] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002411)

**[01:52:07.480] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002415)

**[01:52:08.940] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002416)

**[01:52:12.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002418)

**[01:52:14.420] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002419)

**[01:52:16.840] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002420)

**[01:52:18.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002421)

**[01:52:33.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002427)

**[01:52:35.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002428)

**[01:52:46.620] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-002434)

**[01:52:53.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002437)

in

a

**[01:53:57.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002471)

**[01:54:03.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002472)

**[01:54:48.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002495)

**[01:54:51.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002496)  
law and capacitating

**[01:54:52.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-002497)
