**[03:15:32.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004993)  
Magkaiba yun.

**[03:15:34.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004994)  
Yung maglagay ka ng digital sa some classes and sa some courses, etc.

**[03:15:39.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004995)  
But you do not have a digital curriculum.

**[03:15:44.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004996)  
Same thing, di ba?

**[03:15:45.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004997)  
If you have a newspaper, you have a digital version.

**[03:15:49.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004998)  
If you just put it on the internet or Facebook, it's not a digital version.

**[03:15:55.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-004999)  
Dinigitalize mo lang.

**[03:15:56.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005000)  
Pero it's the same newspaper.

**[03:15:58.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005001)  
That's why if you look at most, di ba?

**[03:16:00.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005002)  
If you look at inquire.net, malilabulitin.net, GMA, it's different than their analog version.

**[03:16:07.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005003)  
Philippine Digital Infrastructure Project, 2.1 billion, foreign-assisted to,

**[03:16:13.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005004)  
ang tinira na lang, 360 million.

**[03:16:15.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005005)  
So akala ko ba importante sa atin na mag-digitalize?

**[03:16:19.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005006)  
E ba't natin tinanggalan ng 1.8 billion ang Philippine Digital Infrastructure Project?

**[03:16:26.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005007)  
No?

**[03:16:26.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005008)  
Panahon ng pagkilos, Philippine Community Resilience Project, World Bank Loan No. 98460.

**[03:16:34.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005009)  
17 billion, binawasan natin ng 5.5 billion.

**[03:16:41.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005010)  
Davao Public Transport Modernization, 15 billion, tinanggal natin ng 5 billion.

**[03:16:50.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005011)  
From 15 billion to 9.9.

**[03:16:52.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005012)  
Davao is three or four times bigger than Metro Manila.

**[03:16:55.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005013)  
We don't want...

**[03:16:56.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005014)  
We don't want the gridlock in Metro Manila to hit them.

**[03:16:59.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005015)  
Eto, maganda to.

**[03:17:00.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005016)  
Laguna Lakeshore Road Network.

**[03:17:03.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005017)  
Dami na pinag-usapan dito.

**[03:17:05.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005018)  
Inaaway ko sa klasa, design na to.

**[03:17:08.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005019)  
At sinabi sa akin ni Secretary Vinci, singiling ko siya,

**[03:17:11.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005020)  
binigyan ko sila ng assignment,

**[03:17:13.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005021)  
hindi ba mas magandang train ang ilagay doon rather than kalye?

**[03:17:16.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005022)  
Sabi niya, wala tayong magagawa, na loan na pagdating niya doon.

**[03:17:19.560] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005023)  
Ang loan is 35 billion, binaba natin to 22.

**[03:17:23.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005024)  
Worse sa house, ginawang 8.2.

**[03:17:25.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005025)  
So, sorry.

**[03:17:26.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005026)  
I know, don't get me wrong.

**[03:17:31.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005027)  
I remember, Sen. JB, I think the Finance Committee was offered to you before

**[03:17:37.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005028)  
and you know how hard this project is and that's why we appreciate, di ba,

**[03:17:43.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005029)  
during Senator Soto's time as Senate President, also yung kay Wyn, etc.

**[03:17:49.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005030)  
It's so hard to have priority projects ililipat mo.

**[03:17:53.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005031)  
But I go back to the principle.

**[03:17:55.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005032)  
Kung well-versed...

**[03:17:56.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005033)  
Well-vetted itong foreign-assisted,

**[03:18:01.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005034)  
bakit tayo ngayon kukuha doon tapos ibibigay sa iba?

**[03:18:05.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005035)  
Na hindi natin sure kung well-vetted yun or hindi.

**[03:18:09.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005036)  
So, can I leave this with you?

**[03:18:10.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005037)  
We have time until the end to take a second look at it.

**[03:18:14.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005038)  
And one, let's agree in principle.

**[03:18:16.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005039)  
Mas maganda nga ba foreign-assisted?

**[03:18:18.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005040)  
Two, if you say yes,

**[03:18:22.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005041)  
bakit natin tatanggalan yung specific projects na yon?

**[03:18:25.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005042)  
Di ba?

**[03:18:26.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005043)  
Actually, Mr. President, Your Honor,

**[03:18:29.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005044)  
ito po, these projects that you enumerated,

**[03:18:33.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005045)  
ang house po nagtanggal niya, no?

**[03:18:35.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005046)  
And these are all, I would say, good foreign-assisted projects.

**[03:18:41.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005047)  
Kaya lang po sila, 130 billion ang total that was removed.

**[03:18:46.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005048)  
And we tried to restore, but we were only able to raise about 87 billion total.

**[03:18:53.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005049)  
So talagang kulang yung na-source natin eh.

**[03:18:56.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005050)  
I agree with you, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:18:59.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005051)  
Theoretically, we're confined to do the gab.

**[03:19:02.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005052)  
But pagdating sa BICAM, you can use anything.

**[03:19:06.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005053)  
You can use Senate version, House version, you can use the NEP.

**[03:19:08.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005054)  
Kasi, for example, Davao Transport Modernization, pareho eh.

**[03:19:13.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005055)  
15 billion yung NEP, 9.9 at 9.9 din House at saka the Senate eh.

**[03:19:20.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005056)  
Yung tama ka, let's say, dun sa Laguna Lake,

**[03:19:26.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005057)  
eto, sa panahon ng pagkilos, yung World Bank,

**[03:19:31.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005058)  
17 billion, 12.1, 12.1 eh.

**[03:19:34.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005059)  
Both House and Senate, 12.1 eh.

**[03:19:36.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005060)  
Philippine Digital, pareho eh, House at Senate.

**[03:19:40.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005061)  
So, ang hindi ko lang na kasi matanggap yung principle,

**[03:19:44.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005062)  
nasasabihin natin, well-vetted yung, huwag natin palitan yun.

**[03:19:49.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005063)  
So now we're saying, kailangan natin maghanap para sa bagong projects na gusto natin lagyan.

**[03:19:54.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005064)  
Pero is it as well-vetted as the other?

**[03:19:56.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005065)  
We all agree, hindi.

**[03:20:00.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005066)  
So why move money to a less efficient program when you have a more efficient program?

**[03:20:07.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005067)  
That's my point there.

**[03:20:08.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005068)  
But I'll be more than happy for you to review, but I'll definitely bring this up again.

**[03:20:14.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005069)  
Can I bring up one point also there, Mr. Chairman?

**[03:20:18.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005070)  
May bagong lumang requirements for infrastructure projects,

**[03:20:23.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005071)  
bago natin ipsama sa, sa ano?

**[03:20:26.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005072)  
Sa JA, na dapat RDC, dapat, et cetera, et cetera, na?

**[03:20:34.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005073)  
So eto, from DPWH daw to.

**[03:20:37.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005074)  
And strictly daw sinusunod ng ating LBRMO.

**[03:20:41.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005075)  
Approved Program of Works, Validated JUTAG, Budget Preparation Compliance,

**[03:20:48.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005076)  
Certificate of Implementability, Master Plan or Feasibility, RDC, et cetera.

**[03:20:54.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005077)  
Baka itong Master Plan at Feasibility ang problema.

**[03:20:56.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005078)  
If you have a 5 billion peso project, pero 3 phases.

**[03:21:02.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005079)  
First year, 1.5 billion, 2025.

**[03:21:06.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005080)  
Second phase, 2026, 1.5 billion, 3 billion.

**[03:21:09.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005081)  
2027, 2 billion, matatapos na.

**[03:21:14.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005082)  
Pero wala sa requirements na to.

**[03:21:17.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005083)  
Ititiwangwang na lang natin yung project na 3 billion na ginastos natin.

**[03:21:22.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005084)  
I'm saying it's a productive project, ha?

**[03:21:24.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005085)  
Never mind.

**[03:21:25.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005086)  
Never mind.

**[03:21:26.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005087)  
Of course, if it's a ghost project, hindi itiwangwang yung 3 billion, ninakaw na.

**[03:21:33.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005088)  
I've talked to a lot of local officials.

**[03:21:36.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005089)  
Hindi sila makakumply sa binigay nitong DP.

**[03:21:39.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005090)  
Pero may ongoing sila.

**[03:21:41.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005091)  
Hospital, Coliseum, Port.

**[03:21:45.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005092)  
So, for me, mas importante ang substance than forma.

**[03:21:50.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005093)  
Baka napakagaling natin pumorma sa budget.

**[03:21:53.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005094)  
Ito ang mga requirements.

**[03:21:54.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005095)  
Sa 2027, we assure you.

**[03:21:56.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005096)  
Walang wastage ang pera eto.

**[03:22:01.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005097)  
Hindi, mawe-waste nga yun kasi pinondohan mo na the last few years.

**[03:22:05.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005098)  
Pero hindi matatapos ngayon because of some bureaucratic problem.

**[03:22:09.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005099)  
So, Mr. Chairman, can the DPWH not submit pag ongoing, then justify it, then to follow yung ku-anuman?

**[03:22:17.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005100)  
I mean, if you already spent 3 billion for 5 billion projects, saka mo gagawa ng feasibility study or master plan.

**[03:22:23.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005101)  
And if these projects were implemented by the...

**[03:22:26.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005102)  
regional or the district engineer,

**[03:22:29.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005103)  
di ba?

**[03:22:30.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005104)  
You don't expect them na suddenly, 2-3 months before, we tell them na ilagay itong requirements na to.

**[03:22:39.660] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005105)  
I told Secretary Vins the same thing.

**[03:22:42.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005106)  
Sabi ko, Secretary Vins, because of the media, kinansel mo lahat ng flood control programs nung top 10, top 15 na mukhang may problema.

**[03:22:52.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005107)  
Sabi ko, pag kinansel mo yan, rather than i-confiscate mo yung bond at patapusin,

**[03:22:56.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005108)  
patapusin mo sila, tiwangwang yan.

**[03:22:59.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005109)  
O, nung budget hearing, inamin niya, tiwangwang.

**[03:23:02.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005110)  
Pero nung una, nung una niyang buwan, sabi niya, hindi, may kukuha niyan.

**[03:23:06.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005111)  
Kami bahala, sasabihin namin sa ibang contractor, kunin niyo yan.

**[03:23:09.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005112)  
Andami natin projects ngayon, nakatiwangwang.

**[03:23:12.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005113)  
So, double whammy, we slowed down, and then hindi usable yung project na iyon.

**[03:23:17.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005114)  
So, I'll give you an example.

**[03:23:18.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005115)  
A hospital that just needs 200 million to be completed, you already spent 800 million.

**[03:23:24.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005116)  
Nakatiwangwang yan.

**[03:23:25.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005117)  
So, double.

**[03:23:26.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005118)  
You don't only lose the 800 million.

**[03:23:29.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005119)  
You don't only save the 200 million.

**[03:23:31.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005120)  
Hindi magamit kasi hindi patapos.

**[03:23:34.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005121)  
So, may I maybe convert this to an appeal.

**[03:23:36.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005122)  
I don't know exactly these projects.

**[03:23:39.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005123)  
Pero ang dami kong kausap, Senator JV, di ba?

**[03:23:43.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005124)  
There are very, very few rules in the world that doesn't have exceptions.

**[03:23:49.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005125)  
Especially if you implement the rule ng apurahan.

**[03:23:52.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005126)  
If you had this rule for two years, and hindi sinunod ngayon,

**[03:23:56.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005127)  
wala kang sisisihin kung hindi yung agency.

**[03:24:00.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005128)  
Di ba?

**[03:24:01.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005129)  
So, ayokong si Sec Lopez naman ngayon.

**[03:24:03.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005130)  
Baka umikot yun.

**[03:24:04.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005131)  
Magmura, magturo.

**[03:24:06.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005132)  
E yung project na to, tapos pag tinignan niya,

**[03:24:09.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005133)  
eh sir, kaya tinutubuan na ng halaman yan,

**[03:24:12.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005134)  
kasi hindi naman pinunduhan ng Congress for 2020.

**[03:24:15.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005135)  
Bakit wala?

**[03:24:16.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005136)  
Pero may pondo siya ng 26, 25, 24.

**[03:24:20.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005137)  
So, if we really want an efficient budget,

**[03:24:22.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005138)  
we have to see the realities on the ground.

**[03:24:25.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005139)  
Not only the...

**[03:24:25.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005140)  
what looks good in media and on paper.

**[03:24:29.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005141)  
So, yun lang appeal ko between now and by come.

**[03:24:33.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005142)  
Allow the LGUs, allow the congressmen.

**[03:24:36.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005143)  
You know, minsan nadidemonize masyado na,

**[03:24:39.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005144)  
congressman, nasisingit ng project.

**[03:24:41.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005145)  
They're there to represent their constituents.

**[03:24:44.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005146)  
So, if I was a congressman now, Senator JV,

**[03:24:46.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005147)  
at may hospital sa Taguig,

**[03:24:48.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005148)  
na, let's say, 1 billion peso hospital,

**[03:24:51.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005149)  
800 million na nagastos,

**[03:24:52.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005150)  
hindi ma-operate without the 200 million,

**[03:24:54.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005151)  
hindi maipasok ngayon sa GAA.

**[03:24:57.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005152)  
Mahiya akong ipasok yun

**[03:24:59.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005153)  
because walang requirement

**[03:25:01.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005154)  
or it's my duty as a congressman to say na,

**[03:25:04.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005155)  
if you were a mayor now,

**[03:25:06.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005156)  
na hindi mo mailapit

**[03:25:08.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005157)  
dahil kagalit mo yung congressman

**[03:25:10.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005158)  
or sa senador mo nilapit,

**[03:25:13.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005159)  
pero yung senador na yun,

**[03:25:14.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005160)  
anong isasagot natin?

**[03:25:15.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005161)  
Ma'am, maganda yung ospital,

**[03:25:17.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005162)  
pero sorry ha, yung binigay na DPWH na papel,

**[03:25:20.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005163)  
kailangan naming sundin.

**[03:25:22.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005164)  
Hindi mapapagamot nung papel na to yung...

**[03:25:24.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005165)  
kayang ipagamot nung ospital na yun.

**[03:25:26.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005166)  
So, may I appeal to you, Mr. Chairman,

**[03:25:28.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005167)  
to take a look at that.

**[03:25:29.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005168)  
I'm not asking na you change the rules.

**[03:25:32.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005169)  
I'm asking you to look at the legitimate exceptions

**[03:25:35.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005170)  
that will bring harm to the 2027 budget.

**[03:25:53.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005171)  
Yes or no, Mr. Chairman?

**[03:25:54.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005172)  
Are you fixed with that?

**[03:25:55.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005173)  
Yes, Mr. President.

**[03:25:57.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005174)  
So, I'm waiting for your answer.

**[03:25:59.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-005175)  
You are a very kind-hearted person,

**[03:50:47.320] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005800)

**[03:50:51.460] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005801)

**[03:50:58.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005802)  
Mr. President, it was proposed that the removal of that,

**[03:51:10.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005803)

**[03:51:18.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005804)

**[03:51:28.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005805)  
of 2026.

**[03:51:30.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005806)

**[03:51:35.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005807)

**[03:51:42.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005808)  
But the DBCC, according to our economic managers,

**[03:51:45.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005809)

**[03:51:53.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005810)

**[03:52:01.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005812)  
since magpupulong na rin lang naman sila within a few weeks

**[03:52:06.260] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005813)

**[03:52:21.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005817)

**[03:52:23.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005818)

**[03:52:28.660] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005819)

**[03:52:35.760] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005820)

**[03:52:48.520] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-005822)

**[03:52:58.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005823)  
Mr. President, according to our DBM Secretary,

**[03:53:14.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005826)

**[03:53:24.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005828)

**[03:53:28.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005829)

**[03:53:58.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005836)

**[03:59:43.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005928)

**[04:18:38.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006389)

**[04:18:49.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006390)

**[04:18:55.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006391)

**[04:19:19.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006395)  
Yes, and DEFA.

**[04:19:20.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006396)  
Was signed on November 14, 2026, covering digital trade, cross-border payments, and data flows.

**[04:19:27.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006397)  
Its implementation could support digital trade and financial services integration subject to the entry into force and domestic implementation.

**[04:19:37.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006398)  
And last but not the least, ito po, nagkaroon sila na, of course, yung APEC Economic Leaders Meeting,

**[04:19:44.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006399)  
the Philippines-Chile Comprehensive Economic Partnership Agreement.

**[04:19:49.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006400)

**[04:19:55.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006401)

**[04:20:03.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006402)

One to two years po.

One to two years.

**[04:21:44.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006419)

**[04:21:49.260] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006421)  
Yes, good sponsor.

**[04:21:50.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006422)

**[04:21:57.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006423)

**[04:22:05.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006424)

**[04:22:16.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006425)  
Mr. President, Your Honor.

**[04:22:17.920] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006426)  
Of course.

**[04:22:19.260] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006427)  
Good sponsor.

**[04:22:20.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006428)

**[04:22:32.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006429)

**[04:22:48.020] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006430)  
At kaya ko po itinanong ito, yung huling tanong ko, Mr. President, good sponsor, dahil kongkretong mga plano na may pondo at malino na mga deadlines ang hinahanap ng ating mga mamamayan.

**[04:23:01.600] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006431)  
Magandang pagkakataon yung binanggit nyo, good sponsor, na DEFA, kung totoong magkakaroon ng mga bagong suki ang mga Pilipinong MSMEs sa loob ng ASEAN.

**[04:23:14.180] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006432)

**[04:23:18.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006433)  
At mga manggagawa, abot kaya at mabilis na internet. At may tuloy-tuloy na suporta din para sa mga negosyanteng gustong mag-export.

**[04:23:28.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006434)  
Kasi gusto natin maging mas productive sa agricultural at manufacturing sectors natin, mag-generate ng surplus dyan.

**[04:23:37.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006435)  
At may export natin para nga maging mas healthy din yung ating balance of trade. Ito ang ilan sa ating kinakailangan para ma-maximize ito ng Pilipinas.

**[04:23:48.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006436)  
At ng industriyang Pilipino.

**[04:23:50.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006437)  
Ma'am, regarding ano, can we go back yung poong may information na yun?

**[04:23:53.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006438)  
Yes, please. Salamat.

**[04:23:54.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006439)  
Yung poong babalikan natin.

**[04:23:55.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006440)  
Opo.

**[04:23:56.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006441)  
So, according to our economic managers that delays in public infra projects have contributed to the slowdown in economic growth.

**[04:24:05.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006442)  
Public construction delays reduce GDP growth by 0.5 percentage point in third quarter 2026.

**[04:24:14.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006443)  
And by 1.6 percentage in the...

**[04:24:18.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006444)  
First three quarters of 2026.

**[04:24:20.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006445)  
So, ito po yung mga naging effect to the GDP growth.

**[04:24:27.440] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006446)  
So, pag sinuma po natin good sponsor, so, naitala na reduction by 2.1% over these four quarters this year.

**[04:24:37.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006447)  
At kung gamitin natin yung per percentage point, bawas ng 38.8 billion.

**[04:24:47.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006448)  
So, sa dalawang percentage point na nabawas ngayong taon, ano na po yun?

**[04:24:55.380] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006449)  
Forgone revenue na or profit for the national economy na mga 76 billion pesos.

**[04:25:10.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006450)  
According to Secretary Balisakan, the growth should be about

**[04:25:17.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006451)  
4.1%, Your Honor, Mr. President.

**[04:25:21.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006452)  
In the first three quarters.

**[04:25:24.360] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006453)  
Of this year, Mr.

**[04:25:26.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006454)  
This year, Mr. President.

**[04:25:27.300] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006455)  
It should have been 4.3.

**[04:25:30.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006456)  
4.1.

**[04:25:31.340] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006457)  
4.1.

**[04:25:34.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006458)  
Instead of 2.5.

**[04:25:36.120] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006459)  
Okay.

**[04:25:36.740] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006460)  
So, halos na kalahati talaga, Mr. Chair.

**[04:25:39.240] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006461)  
So, mukhang sa pagdatapos ko ng pag-interpolate at maraming salamat, good sponsor, Mr. President, sa pagsagot.

**[04:25:47.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006462)  
Mukhang marami na tayong mapapait at mahalagang leksyon ngayong taon na siguro we can't do things the same way next year.

**[04:26:03.620] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006463)  
Tapos, aasa tayo ng ibang resulta.

**[04:26:07.100] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006464)  
So, common sense lang, meron talaga tayong kailangan baguhin sa ating economic playbook.

**[04:26:13.280] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006465)  
And, umaasa po ako at nagko-commit sa chair na,

**[04:26:17.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006466)  
pagsikapan dito sa loob nitong budget instrument,

**[04:26:22.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006467)  
i-reflect na yung ilang mga pagbabago sa economic playbook na ito

**[04:26:27.000] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006468)  
para maka-generate tayo ng ibang resulta sa susunod na taon.

**[04:26:32.220] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006469)  
Dahil kung hindi, kung magmamatigas po tayo na gawin pa rin yung ginawa natin itong taon,

**[04:26:38.880] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006470)  
na nag-generate ng ganitong mga resulta,

**[04:26:41.900] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006471)  
ang ating mga government economic managers na mismo ang nag-ulat sa atin,

**[04:26:46.820] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006472)  
eh di wala tayong aasalan.

**[04:26:47.960] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006473)  
Kundi more of the same.

**[04:26:49.640] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006474)  
And worse, dahil patuloy pa rin yung gera sa labas,

**[04:26:54.780] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006475)

**[04:26:59.680] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006476)

**[04:27:02.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006477)  
But if we also learn not just the negative lessons na nararanasan natin,

**[04:27:07.720] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006478)

**[04:27:11.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006479)

**[04:27:50.040] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006489)

**[04:27:57.700] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006490)  
para maiwasan yung stagflation at makaahon sa kasalukuyang pagdurusa ng ating mga kababayan

**[04:28:04.800] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006491)  
at palakasin na, pagyamanin na yung ating ekonomiya at yung lahat ng mga kababayan natin

**[04:28:12.840] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006492)

**[04:28:14.980] Senator Risa Hontiveros** (asr-006493)

**[04:29:12.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006499)  
Thank you, Mr. President.

**[04:29:19.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006500)  
Good evening, Chairman. Do you need a...

**[04:29:26.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006503)

**[04:29:31.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006504)

**[04:29:34.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006505)

**[04:29:40.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006506)

**[04:29:42.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006509)

**[04:29:47.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006510)

**[04:29:50.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006511)

**[04:29:53.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006512)  
Yes, we're okay.

**[04:29:55.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006513)  
Okay, thank you so much. I don't think I will be that long.

**[04:30:00.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006514)  
I tried to listen to the questions of our colleagues.

**[04:30:03.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006515)

**[04:30:06.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006516)  
Senator Riza, earlier Senator Allan, our other colleagues.

**[04:30:10.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006517)  
So, I may only repeat them in the sense that they're like follow-up questions.

**[04:30:15.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006518)  
But I'll start with Phil Health.

**[04:30:18.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006519)  
Chairman, alam mo na ang tatanungin ko dito.

**[04:30:21.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006520)  
Alam mo na.

**[04:30:22.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006521)  
And my statement will start with, I'd like to thank the chairperson because

**[04:30:33.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006522)  
my interpolation won't be as harsh as it would be if not for the chairman's intervention.

**[04:30:41.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006523)  
So, this is about the syntax funding.

**[04:30:44.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006524)  
And I knew when I wrote the chairman that the syntax deficiency for 2019,

**[04:30:51.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006525)  
I knew that he wouldn't.

**[04:30:52.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006526)  
I knew that he would look into every possibility to fund it.

**[04:30:56.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006527)  
And thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:30:57.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006528)  
I know it's not an easy job.

**[04:30:58.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006529)  
You produced 19 billion pesos to fund the Phil Health deficit in the sense that

**[04:31:07.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006530)  
it should have been in the NEP and it was not in the NEP.

**[04:31:10.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006531)  
And you found a way to fund this.

**[04:31:13.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006532)  
So, thank you so much for that, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:31:16.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006533)  
But I still really need this not to be the problem of the legislature.

**[04:31:22.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006534)  
So, I will go back to the hearing held just a few months ago in August

**[04:31:27.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006535)  
when I asked the DBM Secretary who was very new at that time.

**[04:31:33.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006536)  
So, I was quite lenient naman po with the need of the Secretary to find out what's going on.

**[04:31:41.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006537)  
And I said, why is this not in the NEP?

**[04:31:44.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006538)  
This is an earmarked fund that every year, I along with other colleagues like Senator Loren and Senator JV,

**[04:31:52.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006539)  
have to look into.

**[04:31:54.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006540)  
So, that question was left hanging because I gave the Secretary the opportunity

**[04:32:00.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006541)  
because he asked for time to discuss with Phil Health.

**[04:32:03.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006542)  
Pero, this has nothing to do with Phil Health because by law, binigay na yung pondo na yan sa Phil Health.

**[04:32:10.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006543)  
Whether Phil Health is making good use of it, great use of it, poor use of it,

**[04:32:16.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006544)  
is some other issue that we have to deal with.

**[04:32:18.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006545)  
So, may I get a quick answer on that, Mr. Chairman?

**[04:32:21.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006546)  
Kasi alam ko.

**[04:32:22.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006547)  
Na-concern din ito ng Chairperson natin.

**[04:32:29.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006548)

**[04:32:33.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006549)

**[04:32:42.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006551)  
because according to him, that the reference for the release of the earmarked funds

**[04:32:50.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006552)

**[04:32:52.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006553)  
A certification from the Bureau of...

**[04:32:54.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006554)

**[04:32:58.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006555)

**[04:33:08.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006558)  
Your Honor, according to him, that...

**[04:33:10.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006559)  
That is what he means.

**[04:33:11.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006560)  
Yes, Mr. President.

**[04:33:13.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006561)

**[04:33:14.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006562)  
delayed daw po yung issuance on certification.

**[04:33:16.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006563)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:33:20.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006564)

**[04:33:33.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006567)

**[04:33:37.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006568)

**[04:33:42.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006569)  
first quarter, and then the certification comes out...

**[04:33:48.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006570)  
comes out by the time...

**[04:33:52.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006571)

**[04:50:34.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006905)  
The reason I bring it up here is because after today,

**[04:50:40.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006906)  
we will be dealing with the individual budgets of different agencies.

**[04:50:44.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006907)  
And we will receive individualized responses.

**[04:50:48.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006908)  
So, I want to ask questions with a whole-of-government approach.

**[04:50:56.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006909)  
So, DOST and PAGASA already warned us of this El Nino that will hit us...

**[04:51:01.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006910)  
that has already hit us between September and December 2026,

**[04:51:05.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006911)  
and then will persist through the first half of 2027.

**[04:51:08.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006912)  
In 2023, PBBM ordered the creation of a coordinating council led by NDRRMC.

**[04:51:16.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006913)  
2023.

**[04:51:18.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006914)  
Based on the National Action Plan for El Nino, at that time, the government identified sectors

**[04:51:24.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006915)  
where plans and activities are being laid out to mitigate the effects of El Nino.

**[04:51:28.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006916)  
Water, food, power, health, and public safety.

**[04:51:32.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006917)  
That was 2023.

**[04:51:34.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006918)  
Today, it is 2026.

**[04:51:35.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006919)  
The government announced that PBBM will lead the task force meetings

**[04:51:40.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006920)  
and directly issue orders on the course of action to be taken.

**[04:51:48.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006921)  
So, may we know more details about this?

**[04:51:53.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006922)  
Is there an action plan?

**[04:51:55.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006923)  
Because from the hearings we had,

**[04:51:59.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006924)  
medyo kanya-kanyang action plan.

**[04:52:18.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006949)  
For the record, the Office of Civil Defense,

**[04:52:26.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006950)  
I think a month ago or two,

**[04:52:28.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006951)  
reported that the government is set to implement its El Nino action plan.

**[04:52:32.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006952)  
Maybe we can see this so that when we call the other agencies involved,

**[04:52:39.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006953)  
because DOH is involved, DA is involved, DSWD is involved,

**[04:52:42.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006954)  
we can also try to help ensure that they are coordinated.

**[04:52:48.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006955)  
Are you correct that it's OCD that's heading the task force,

**[04:52:57.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006956)  
according to the economic managers?

**[04:53:01.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006957)  
But we have included in the budget, in the Senate,

**[04:53:08.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006958)  
we have included in some programs like the assistance to farmers and fisher folks,

**[04:53:18.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006959)  
under the DA, direct assistance, about 5 billion.

**[04:53:23.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006960)  
We allotted 5 billion.

**[04:53:28.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006961)  
There, the buffer for NFA, there's a buffer stocking program of 11.2 billion.

**[04:53:38.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006962)  
And then also construction, rehabilitation of water supply, about 7.3 billion.

**[04:53:45.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006963)  
So for DPWH.

**[04:53:48.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006964)  
And rainwater collector systems.

**[04:53:52.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006965)  
And then the NDRM, the National Disaster Risk Reduction Management,

**[04:53:58.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006966)  
there's a 45.7 billion calamity fund.

**[04:54:05.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006967)  
But we reduced it by, the House reduced it to 35

**[04:54:09.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006968)  
because nilagay nila sa DA, direct assistance to farmers and fisher folks.

**[04:54:14.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006969)  
Thank you.

**[04:54:18.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006970)  
It's okay, Mr. President, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:54:27.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006972)

**[04:55:13.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006983)

**[04:55:18.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006984)

**[04:55:21.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006985)

**[04:55:24.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006986)  
And just for that record, Your Honor, Mr. President,

**[04:55:27.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006987)  
for NEP level, a total of 104 billion is allotted for El Nino preparation.

**[04:55:39.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006988)  
Sorry again, can you repeat it?

**[04:55:41.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006989)  
104 billion.

**[04:55:45.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006990)  
What is it?

**[04:55:45.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006991)  
In distributed different agencies.

**[04:55:48.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006992)

**[04:55:59.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006993)  
So we'll just give you a copy, Your Honor, Mr. President,

**[04:56:04.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-006994)  
for the El Nino preparedness response, the proposed programs and activities and projects.

**[04:56:10.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006995)  
Okay, thank you so much for that, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:56:18.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006996)  
I'll repeat something I always repeat in every budget season.

**[04:56:24.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006997)  
The Senate has a committee on sustainable development goals, innovations, and futures thinking.

**[04:56:28.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006998)  
And I think I mentioned this during the hearing, that when I chaired the budget of various committees,

**[04:56:37.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-006999)  
I'd like to think I was able to convince them on the importance of having a futures thinking office.

**[04:56:44.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007000)  
So if, unfortunately, I think DA doesn't have it.

**[04:56:48.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007001)  
I'm not aware that DA has.

**[04:56:52.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007002)  
And so when we are confronted with calamities like this, it's not that difficult to predict.

**[04:56:59.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007003)  
It really isn't.

**[04:57:02.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007004)  
That really is the purpose of having a futures thinking office that doesn't do anything but solely predict the future.

**[04:57:08.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007005)  
Why do we have to do that?

**[04:57:09.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007006)  
Well, it is our job to create programs and to fund programs that will ensure intergenerational fairness.

**[04:57:18.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007007)  
So again, I said this before, this is not new.

**[04:57:20.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007008)  
Some countries prepare seven generations ahead.

**[04:57:23.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007009)  
But when you're confronted with a calamity, it's hard to think of those seven generations ahead

**[04:57:28.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007010)  
because you're thinking of the survival of every family that's living right now today in 2026.

**[04:57:34.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007011)  
And that's why we need such an office, I think maybe under NEDA or, I don't know, a coordinating body

**[04:57:43.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007012)  
so that we can be sure that we are not sacrificing the future.

**[04:57:48.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007013)  
For the present need and vice versa, we're not putting all our funding in the future and sacrificing the present.

**[04:57:55.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007014)  
So I don't know.

**[04:57:56.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007015)  
If I ask now, what is the balancing act?

**[04:57:59.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007016)  
Can I get an answer?

**[04:58:00.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007017)  
Like, what are we, do we know?

**[04:58:03.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007018)  
What kind of funding are we doing that is an investment in the future versus investing today?

**[04:58:10.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007019)  
And my favorite agency as the chairman also is health, right?

**[04:58:14.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007020)

**[04:58:17.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007021)  
Clear tayo?

**[04:58:18.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007022)  
Diba?

**[04:58:19.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007023)  
It's not something that will save a life of a child with malaria right now, and not malaria, measles right now.

**[04:58:26.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007024)  
It's too late to give them the measles vaccination.

**[04:58:29.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007025)  
So that's the future of this child's life.

**[04:58:31.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007026)  
We should know how much of our funding goes into future planning and how much is stopgap,

**[04:58:37.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007027)  
which on the one hand, you might consider ayuda.

**[04:58:40.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007028)

**[04:58:41.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007029)

**[04:58:48.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007030)  
that's not ayuda.

**[04:58:49.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007031)

**[04:58:53.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007032)

**[04:59:01.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007033)  
you're banking these seeds, and so on and so forth.

**[04:59:03.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007034)  
Do we even have that kind of data, Mr. President, Mr. Chairman?

**[04:59:44.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007037)  
Your Honor, Mr. President,

**[04:59:46.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007049)

**[04:59:56.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007050)

**[05:00:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007051)

**[05:00:07.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007052)

**[05:00:24.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007053)

**[05:00:27.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007054)

**[05:00:32.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007055)

**[05:00:40.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007056)

**[05:00:45.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007057)

**[05:00:49.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007058)

**[05:00:57.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007059)

**[05:01:01.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007060)

In the last SONA, the President said, well, ordered na tanggalin na yung systems laws

**[05:16:34.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007269)

**[05:16:38.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007270)  
So because I handled the budget of DOE, ERC, NEA, and the like,

**[05:16:46.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007271)  
sabi ni DOE, wala na siyang ibang hihingi na budgetary amendment,

**[05:16:51.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007272)

**[05:16:58.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007273)  
which, according to NEA, will be used directly to implement these instructions of the President,

**[05:17:07.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007274)

**[05:17:12.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007275)

**[05:17:19.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007276)

**[05:17:24.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007277)  
So, the Chairman...

**[05:17:28.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007278)

**[05:17:32.580] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007279)

**[05:17:35.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007280)

**[05:17:42.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007281)  
and so, I don't know if the economic team has any input on this,

**[05:17:48.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007282)

**[05:17:53.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007283)  
So, now, when I talk to NEA, and I promise them that I will do whatever I can,

**[05:17:58.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007284)  
because all over the country, ang dami-dami pa rin brown-out.

**[05:18:03.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007285)

**[05:18:07.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007286)

**[05:18:12.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007287)  
they live with this, you know, unreliable electricity, intermittent brown-outs,

**[05:18:20.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007288)

**[05:18:27.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007289)  
So, now, NEA...

**[05:18:28.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007290)

**[05:18:34.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007291)  
So, that is my task to help them find some kind of support for this, Mr. President.

**[05:18:43.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007292)  
And I'm bringing this to the attention of NEDA, because instructions nga ito ng Presidente.

**[05:18:49.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007293)  
So, pag sinabing, oh, hindi pinasaya ng Congress, well, alam ko na wala yan sa NEP.

**[05:18:56.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007294)  
Wala sa NEP.

**[05:18:57.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007295)  
Kasi nga, by the time...

**[05:18:58.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007296)  
That SONA was delivered, wala pang NEP.

**[05:19:01.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007297)

**[05:19:10.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007298)  
And this matters, because these are the small entrepreneurs, the MSMEs,

**[05:19:28.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007301)

**[05:21:33.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007329)  
Your Honor, Mr. President, according to Secretary, DOF Secretary, the Economic Managers,

**[05:21:51.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007333)

**[05:22:25.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007340)

**[05:22:30.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007342)

**[05:22:38.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007343)  
Repayment.

No, their history is 99%.

**[05:22:40.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007345)

**[05:22:42.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007346)

**[05:22:45.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007347)

**[05:22:58.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007351)

**[05:23:01.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007352)

**[05:23:03.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007353)

Okay.

**[05:23:37.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007359)  
Anyway, I'm just letting the body know nga.

**[05:23:39.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007360)

**[05:23:46.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007361)

Unfunded laws.

**[05:23:56.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007364)  
We have 132 completely unfunded laws.

**[05:24:00.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007365)  
And we have 156 partially funded laws.

**[05:24:03.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007366)  
This is based on the Congressional Policy and Budget Research of the House of Representatives.

**[05:24:08.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007367)  
So, one of the examples I'll give is the National Nutrition Bill, which we just passed.

**[05:24:15.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007368)  
National Children's Nutrition Bill, which we just passed.

**[05:24:19.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007369)

**[05:24:26.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007370)  
Pet project of Senator Allan, pet project of Senator Grace, Senator Joel, myself.

**[05:24:32.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007371)  
Every year, Senator Sherwin, every year dinadagdagan natin yan.

**[05:24:36.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007372)

**[05:24:39.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007373)

**[05:24:48.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007374)

**[05:25:00.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007375)

**[05:25:05.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007376)  
I'm just saying, I know it's work in progress.

**[05:25:08.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007377)

**[05:25:12.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007378)

**[05:25:14.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007379)

**[05:25:33.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-007382)

**[05:57:40.580] Senator Mark A. Villar** (asr-008023)

**[05:57:44.920] Senator Mark A. Villar** (asr-008025)

**[05:57:45.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008026)

**[05:57:55.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008030)

**[05:57:59.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008032)

**[05:58:02.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008034)

**[05:58:15.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008035)

**[05:58:24.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008036)

**[05:58:33.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008037)

**[05:58:47.720] Senator Mark A. Villar** (asr-008038)

**[05:59:04.640] Senator Mark A. Villar** (asr-008040)

**[05:59:15.840] Senator Mark A. Villar** (asr-008041)

**[05:59:24.520] Senator Mark A. Villar** (asr-008042)

**[05:59:54.520] Senator Mark A. Villar** (asr-008045)

the,

**[06:24:51.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008737)  
so ito po yung mga

**[06:24:51.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008738)

Your Honor,

**[06:24:54.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008741)  
Mr. President,

**[06:24:55.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008742)  
yung de-

**[06:24:55.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008743)  
um,

**[06:24:56.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008744)  
iyon daw,

**[06:24:56.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008745)

**[06:25:11.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008747)

**[06:25:16.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008748)

**[06:25:21.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008749)  
It's on the difference in purchasing power in the different regions.

**[06:25:27.460] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008750)  
Yeah, medyo long term po yan. Ano yung stopgap? Yung immediately using the 2027 budget.

**[06:25:39.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008751)  
Siguro LGSF, empowerment ng mga local government units.

**[06:25:46.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008752)  
Well, that's part of it because...

**[06:25:48.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008753)  
Local Government Support Fund.

**[06:25:51.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008754)  
That could be a good stopgap measure to address the inequitable distribution ng wealth sa mga regions.

**[06:26:05.380] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008755)  
However, ito yung problema.

**[06:26:08.600] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008756)  
Yung Local Government Support Fund, tumaas po ito. Nag-triple na nga ito starting 2026.

**[06:26:15.820] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008757)  
From the 2024 to 2025 level.

**[06:26:21.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008758)  
Now, under the NEP, itong LGSF, nasa 58.5 billion.

**[06:26:32.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008759)  
Yung 2027, House General Appropriations Bill, nag-despike ito ng 62.6 billion.

**[06:26:43.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008760)  
Wala pa akong reklamo dyan.

**[06:26:45.380] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008761)  
2027 sa Senate, sa Committee Report, naging 61.023.

**[06:26:51.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008762)  
Well and good.

**[06:26:53.940] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008763)  
Ang problema lang, ang bulk ng LGSF napunta sa PALGU.

**[06:26:59.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008764)  
Yung PALGU is a lump-sum appropriation.

**[06:27:03.820] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008765)  
Wala po itong... May mga items, pero walang specific allocations.

**[06:27:09.060] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008766)  
Ito yung menu. Nandyan din yung menu ng PALGU.

**[06:27:13.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008767)  
What I'm trying to drive at, Mr. President, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[06:27:18.060] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008768)  
bakit binawasan natin yung Growth Equity Fund?

**[06:27:21.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008769)  
Eh, ito kasi yung makakatulo sa mga poor municipalities, provinces, and even barangays.

**[06:27:28.640] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008770)  
Kasi pagka Growth Equity Fund, di ba ito yung binibigay lang sa mga fourth and fifth class municipalities and provinces?

**[06:27:37.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008771)  
Bakit yung pa yung binawasan natin? Eh, yun ang medyo may mga kriteriya.

**[06:27:43.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008772)  
Ang pinambakan natin yung PALGU na medyo sabi kong lump-sum, ano?

**[06:27:47.840] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008773)  
At under the PALGU,

**[06:27:51.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008774)  
andito pa rin yung mga kasamang yung multi-purpose buildings, ano?

**[06:27:59.420] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008775)  
Merong menu, pero wala namang specific items or walang amounts, walang allocations.

**[06:28:07.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008776)  
So, if I may suggest, at the proper time, para makauwi na rin yung ating mga sekretaris, saka yung mga staff,

**[06:28:16.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008777)  
siguro at the proper time,

**[06:28:18.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008778)  
bigyan natin ang mas malaking pondo yung...

**[06:28:21.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008779)  
Growth Equity Fund.

**[06:28:23.520] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008780)  
Kasi ito yung mag-i-equalize ng inequitable distribution ng biyaya, ika nga, from the national government.

**[06:28:34.300] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008781)  
Would you agree, Distinguished Sponsor?

**[06:28:38.840] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008782)  
Huwag natin itambak sa PALGU?

**[06:28:40.500] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008783)  
Yes, we agree, Your Honor, Mr. President.

**[06:28:42.460] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008784)  
So, I'm allowed to introduce amendments in that regard?

**[06:28:46.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008785)  
Yes.

**[06:28:47.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008786)  
And you will accept?

**[06:28:50.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008787)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[06:28:51.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-008788)  
As long as it will improve, ano, yung pong baka...

**[06:28:56.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008789)  
Okay.

**[06:28:58.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008790)  
So, with that assurance, pwede na tayo muwi.

**[06:29:02.920] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-008791)  
Thank you very much, Mr. President.
